### [94] [SN-ASMO: Satellite-Navigation Array Spatial-Manifold Precise Observation Theory A Mathematical Foundation for Observation Formation, Unified U(1) Geometry, Intrinsic Information, and Preservation of the RTK Integer Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13611)
*Xianwei Meng*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出 SN-ASMO 框架，解决接收机主动参与观测形成（阵列几何、通道状态、权值与信号处理规则）时，载波观测量如何在本征传播过程、连续相位与整数模糊度结构均不改变的前提下保持客观一致的基础性问题；通过严格分离"传播引起"与"观测者引起"的变化，实现抗干扰、高动态与载波相位 RTK 在观测形成层面的解耦，并给出可复现、可证伪的验证协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压制式干扰、接收平台高动态运动以及载波相位 RTK 使卫星导航面临一个根本问题：当接收机通过阵列几何、通道状态、权重和信号处理规则主动参与观测形成时，所产生的载波观测量如何仍能表征同一客观传播过程，同时保持相位连续与整数模糊度结构。物理世界不会因观测者重构而改变，但观测世界中的复现会改变；若不严格区分传播引起与观测者引起的变异，抗干扰、高动态与精密载波相位定位将在观测形成层面发生结构性耦合。

Method: SN-ASMO 将观测形成、参考协方差、精确冗余量商（exact nuisance quotients）、同源传输（same-source transport）、本征统计信息与 RTK 整数结构保持统一在一个数学封闭且物理可检验的框架中，并设计了针对静态干扰、动态旋转、支路间定时失配、近零响应、RF 重放与 RTK 整数风险验证的可复现、可证伪协议。

Result: 该框架在数学上封闭、物理上可检验地实现了观测形成层面的统一，使载波观测量能够在接收策略主动变化时仍保持连续相位与整数模糊度结构，并提供了多种对抗场景与 RTK 整数风险下的验证协议作为支撑。

Conclusion: SN-ASMO 的核心创新在于首次将观测形成、参考协方差、精确冗余量商、同源传输、本征统计信息与 RTK 整数结构保持纳入一个统一框架，从而在原理层面回答了接收机主动参与观测形成时载波观测量客观性与整数保持的根本问题，为抗干扰、高动态与精密定位的联合设计提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Suppressive interference, high-dynamic motion of the receiving platform, and carrier-phase RTK lead satellite navigation to one fundamental question: when the receiver actively participates in observation formation through its array geometry, channel states, weights, and signal-processing rules, how can the resulting carrier observation continue to represent the same objective propagation process while preserving continuous phase and the integer-ambiguity structure? The physical world is not changed by a reconfiguration of the observer, but its complex representation in the observation world is. Without a strict separation between propagation-induced and observer-induced variations, anti-jamming, high dynamics, and precise carrier-phase positioning become structurally coupled at the observation-formation level.
  Finally, reproducible and falsifiable protocols are provided for static interference, dynamic rotation, inter-branch timing mismatch, near-null response, RF replay, and RTK integer-risk validation. The scoped novelty of SN-ASMO is the unification of observation formation, reference covariance, exact nuisance quotients, same-source transport, intrinsic statistical information, and preservation of the RTK integer structure in one mathematically closed and physically testable framework.

</details>


### [95] [A Survey of Typical-Cell Volume Distributions in Poisson--Voronoi and Poisson--Delaunay Tessellations: Analytical Theory, High-Dimensional Limits, and Wireless Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13615)
*Minghua Xia,Tian Shi,Wenkunn Wen*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了由泊松点过程诱导的Poisson-Voronoi与Poisson-Delaunay剖分的典型胞元体积分布。作者指出文献在这两类对偶剖分上存在显著的分析不对称性：Poisson-Delaunay单纯形体积已有显式PDF/CDF/矩公式（基于Mellin变换与Meijer G函数），而Poisson-Voronoi胞元体积虽有条件Gamma结构的精确表示，但归一化形状律与无界面数混合分布仍不显式，缺乏无条件的闭式分布。文章回顾主要分析方法、综合精确与近似结果、总结高维极限，并讨论无线网络应用（负载建模、协作传输、三维架构）及若干开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Poisson-Voronoi与Poisson-Delaunay剖分是随机几何、计算几何等领域的基础对偶模型，其典型胞元体积分布是建模覆盖、负载、连通性等系统特征的关键输入。然而，文献中这两类模型的体积分布在解析性上严重不对等：Delaunay侧已有维度显式的闭式结果，而Voronoi侧仅有条件表达式且无条件闭式分布缺失，实际建模多依赖仿真与经验近似。这种不平衡促使作者撰写此综述，以检视并弥合二者之间的分析差距。

Method: 这是一篇综述性（survey）研究。作者对Poisson-Voronoi与Poisson-Delaunay剖分的典型胞元体积分布文献进行系统梳理：(1) 综述主要分析方法（如Mellin变换、Meijer G函数、尺度-形状因子分解等）；(2) 综合精确与近似结果，包括Voronoi侧基于条件Gamma结构的一般维数精确表示，以及Delaunay侧维度显式的PDF、CDF与矩公式；(3) 总结新兴的高维极限行为；(4) 讨论无线网络中的实际应用场景，并归纳开放问题。

Result: 本文揭示并阐明了Poisson-Voronoi与Poisson-Delaunay胞元体积分布之间的分析不对称性：Delaunay单纯形体积拥有完整、显式、维度显式的概率分布刻画（Mellin变换与Meijer G函数形式），而Voronoi胞元体积仅获得条件Gamma结构的一般维数精确表示，其归一化形状律与无界多面体面数混合分布仍为隐式，无条件闭式分布不可得。综述还整合了近似的可处理结果与高维极限，并将这些分布成果映射到无线网络（负载建模、协作传输、三维架构）等应用上，最终明确指出多个开放问题。

Conclusion: Poisson-Voronoi胞元体积的无条件分布仍是核心未解问题，需进一步发展以获得与Delaunay侧相当的解析完备性。未来研究应关注：无条件Poisson-Voronoi分布、非泊松空间模型、数据驱动的几何推断，以及维度感知的建模方法。该综述既系统整合了现有分析工具箱，也为弥合两类对偶剖分之间的理论差距提供了清晰的研究路线图。

Abstract: Random spatial tessellations generated by point processes provide fundamental models for proximity, space partitioning, and local geometry in stochastic systems. Poisson--Voronoi and Poisson--Delaunay tessellations induced by homogeneous Poisson point processes form a canonical dual pair used in stochastic geometry, computational geometry, spatial statistics, and wireless-network analysis. Their typical-cell volume distributions provide important geometric inputs for modeling coverage, traffic load, clustering, connectivity, and other system characteristics. Despite extensive study, the literature remains analytically asymmetric. For Poisson--Voronoi cell volumes, exact integral representations exist in certain planar settings, while a recent scale--shape factorization provides an exact general-dimensional representation with conditional Gamma structure. However, the normalized shape laws and unbounded facet-count mixture remain implicit, and tractable unconditional closed-form distributions are unavailable. Practical modeling therefore relies largely on simulation, moment characterizations, and empirical approximations. By contrast, Poisson--Delaunay simplex volumes admit dimension-explicit PDFs, CDFs, and moment formulas derived through Mellin-transform analysis and Meijer's \(G\)-function representations. Motivated by this contrast, this paper surveys typical-cell volume distributions in Poisson--Voronoi and Poisson--Delaunay tessellations. We review the main analytical methods, synthesize exact and approximate results, summarize emerging high-dimensional limits, and discuss wireless-network applications, including load modeling, cooperative transmission, and three-dimensional architectures. We also identify open problems concerning unconditional Poisson--Voronoi distributions, non-Poisson spatial models, data-driven geometric inference, and dimension-aware network modeling.

</details>


### [96] [Energy-Aware Compression-Computation Co-Adaptation for Latency Minimization in Multi-User Semantic Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13632)
*Loc X. Nguyen,Yumin Park,Avi Deb Raha,Huy Q. Le,Zhu Han,Eui-Nam Huh,Choong Seon Hong*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出能量感知的压缩-计算协同自适应（CoCo）框架，解决DeepJSCC语义通信中用户异构性（QoS期望、信道条件、本地能量差异）问题，通过基站传输更长信号或本地卸载、用户为去噪分配能量，将问题分解为参数优化与资源分配两个子问题，并验证其相对仅速率自适应或仅去噪具有更低总时延。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DeepJSCC语义通信虽在低信道带宽比下能提供高感知质量，但难以适配用户在通信信道质量、期望QoS目标和可用本地能量方面的异构性，需要显式建模并解决这一异构性问题。

Method: 显式刻画用户设备在期望QoS、信道条件和本地能量上的异构性并建立数学模型；提出能量感知的压缩-计算协同自适应（CoCo）框架，基站可通过发送更长信号或卸载到本地设备满足QoS，用户需投入能量对信号去噪以恢复高保真潜在特征；将问题分解为参数优化与资源分配两个子问题，分别采用鲁棒编解码器（无需重训即可适配多种压缩率与信道噪声）和贪心子载波分配算法。

Result: 在加性高斯白噪声信道下的标准图像数据集上，CoCo相比仅速率自适应的DeepJSCC或仅去噪方案降低了总时延，并能确保每个个体用户的QoS需求得到满足。

Conclusion: CoCo通过压缩与计算的协同自适应，有效兼顾用户异构的QoS、信道与能量约束，在保持语义通信优势的同时提升系统整体时延性能，为异构无线环境中的DeepJSCC提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Deep joint source-channel coding-enabled (DeepJSCC) semantic communication (SemCom) has excelled at delivering high perceptual quality at low channel-bandwidth ratios, which positions it as a pillar for next-generation wireless networks. However, the existing works have difficulty accommodating user heterogeneity in terms of communication channel quality, expected quality-of-service (QoS) targets, and the available local energy. Therefore, in this paper, we explicitly reflect the heterogeneity of user devices in terms of the differences in expected QoS, channel condition, and local energy, and then mathematically formulate the problem. Next, we propose an energy-aware compression-computation co-adaptation (CoCo) framework, in which the base station can meet the expected user QoS by transmitting a longer signal or offloading the task to a local device. The user has to dedicate energy to denoising the signal to recover higher-fidelity latent features before feeding it to the semantic decoder. To solve the formulated problem, we first decompose it into two sub-problems: parameter optimization and resource allocation problems. Specifically, we propose a robust codec that effectively works under a diversity of compression rates and channel noise without re-training, while the greedy sub-carrier allocation lowers the communication time. Finally, we present simulation results on standard image datasets over additive white Gaussian noise to demonstrate the effectiveness of CoCo, which reduces total latency relative to rate-only adaptive DeepJSCC or denoising-only, thereby ensuring the demands of each individual user are met.

</details>


### [97] [Optimal Power Allocation and AI Receiver Design for Superimposed DMRS and Data Transmission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13809)
*Sha Hu,Zhongwang Fu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究OFDM-MIMO系统中叠加解调参考符号（SI-DMRS）与数据叠加传输的方案，并设计了基于Transformer编码器、融合迭代信道估计与检测（ICED）结构的AI接收机，在提升频谱效率（SE）方面优于传统非重叠DMRS系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DMRS与数据不重叠的传输方式占用额外资源，频谱效率受限。为提升频谱效率，本文探索SI-DMRS（DMRS与数据叠加）传输，并需要解决叠加传输带来的信道估计（CE）与MIMO检测（MD）相互耦合、误差迭代恶化的问题。

Method: 首先，推导了一个分析框架，表征ICED过程中信道估计与MIMO检测的均方误差（MSE）之间的迭代行为，并据此优化DMRS与数据符号之间的功率分配和导频图案。其次，设计了一种基于Transformer编码器、内嵌ICED结构的AI接收机（AI-ICED），用于SI-DMRS传输。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的AI-ICED接收机结合SI-DMRS传输，相比使用非重叠DMRS与数据的传统系统，能够有效提升频谱效率（SE）。

Conclusion: 通过理论分析框架指导的资源配置优化与基于Transformer的AI接收机相结合，SI-DMRS方案在提升系统频谱效率方面具有明显优势，为未来无线通信系统的高效参考信号设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider transmissions with superimposed (SI) demodulation-reference-symbol (DMRS) and data in orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (OFDM) based multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) systems. First, we derive an analytical framework to characterize the iterative behavior between the mean-square errors (MSEs) of channel estimation (CE) and MIMO detection (MD) within an iterative CE and detection (ICED) process. This framework is subsequently utilized to optimize power allocation and pilot patterns between the DMRS and data symbols for SI-DMRS transmission. Second, we design an artificial intelligence (AI) based receiver built upon Transformer encoders for SI-DMRS transmissions, which incorporates an iterative CE and detection (ICED) structure. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed AI-ICED receiver, combined with SI-DMRS, effectively increases spectral efficiency (SE) compared to conventional systems using non-overlapped DMRS and data symbols.

</details>


### [98] [The Capacity Region of the Multiple Access Channel with Non-Signaling Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13860)
*Yuhang Yao,Syed A. Jafar*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文完全刻画了K发送者离散无记忆多址接入信道（MAC）在非信号（NS）辅助下的容量区域，其形式与经典MAC容量域相同，仅允许输入分布在发送者间任意相关；并证明NS辅助的容量增益因子可任意接近K，结合先前工作的上界，确认K是实现NS辅助乘法增益的极值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前Fawzi和Fermé对K=2发送者的情况建立了外边界，但K发送者情形下的NS辅助容量区域是否具有相同形式仍不明确；同时需要解决NS辅助带来的容量增益的最大可能取值问题，以理解非信号资源在多用户信道中的极限作用。

Method: 在理论上将K=2的结论推广到任意K，给出NS辅助容量区域与经典MAC容量域同构的完整刻画，即仅放宽输入分布在发送者间独立性的约束；同时构造K发送者MAC实例，展示NS辅助的乘法增益可任意接近K，并利用先前工作的上界论证K为该增益的极值。

Result: 证明了K发送者NS辅助MAC的容量区域取“任意相关输入分布”的形式，与Fawzi和Fermé的K=2外边界向K发送者的自然推广一致；并通过示例说明NS辅助的乘法增益可任意接近K，结合已有上界确立K为增益的极值。

Conclusion: NS辅助不会改变MAC容量区域的整体结构，仅移除发送者间的独立性限制；其最大乘法增益为K，标志着非信号辅助在K发送者MAC中的能力极限。

Abstract: The capacity region of the $K$-sender discrete memoryless multiple access channel (MAC) is fully characterized when non-signaling (NS) assistance is available to all $K$ transmitters and the receiver. It is shown to have the same form as the classical capacity region of the MAC, except that the input distribution is allowed to be arbitrarily dependent across the senders. In particular, the NS-assisted capacity region matches the natural generalization to $K$ senders of an outer bound that was previously established by Fawzi and Fermé for $K=2$ senders. Additionally, we provide examples of $K$-sender MACs where the multiplicative gain in capacity from NS-assistance is arbitrarily close to $K$. Combined with an upper bound from prior work, this establishes $K$ as the extremal value of the multiplicative gain from NS-assistance across all $K$-sender MAC settings.

</details>


### [99] [New lower bounds for constant-weight codes via seeded bit-swap tabu search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13906)
*William Echols*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文利用带种子初始化的比特交换禁忌搜索算法，为二元等重码最大规模A(n,d,w)构造了124个新下界，并通过加强A(n,8,8)在n∈{32,33,34,37}的下界，同时改进了相应维数下的亲吻数下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二元等重码的最大规模A(n,d,w)是组合编码理论中的核心问题，其精确值通常未知，需要通过构造法获得下界。同时，A(n,8,8)的更强界可直接用于改进高维亲吻数（kissing numbers）的下界，因此具有重要的理论价值与关联意义。

Method: 采用启发式搜索方法：以种子初始化（seeded initialization）生成候选码集，并使用比特交换禁忌搜索（bit-swap tabu search）在解空间中迭代优化，以构造满足参数(n,d,w)约束且规模尽可能大的合法码。

Result: 发现124个新构造，改进了现有A(n,d,w)的下界；作为推论，对n∈{32,33,34,37}的A(n,8,8)得到更强下界，进而改进了亲吻数τ_32、τ_33、τ_34和τ_37的下界。

Conclusion: 所提出的启发式搜索方法能有效构造高质量二元等重码，显著提升相关参数的下界，并通过组合关联带来高维亲吻数下界的改进，表明该方法在该类优化构造问题中具有实用性与推广价值。

Abstract: A binary constant-weight code is a set of binary words of length $n$ such that each word has exactly weight $w$ and is at least Hamming distance $d$ from every other word in the set. $A(n,d,w)$ denotes the maximum size of a binary constant-weight code with parameters $(n,d,w)$. Using seeded initialization with bit-swap tabu search, we found 124 new constructions that improve existing lower bounds for $A(n,d,w)$. As a corollary of stronger bounds on $A(n,8,8)$ for $n \in \{ 32,33,34,37 \}$, we also improve lower bounds on kissing numbers $τ_{32}$, $τ_{33}$, $τ_{34}$, and $τ_{37}$.

</details>


### [100] [A Structural Characterization of Entropy Functionals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13917)
*Daniel Lazarev*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文在测度论框架下刻画“可容许”的熵泛函：当输入测度关于参考测度绝对连续时，前者的熵必须不超过后者。结合广义均值复合，作者完整表征了所有此类熵泛函，并依据均值生成元、熵尺度与可加性条件建立四级层级；其核心判据（g(1/t) 的严格凸/凹性）同时等价于 Csiszár f-散度生成元的严格凸性，从而在马尔可夫核下给出带精确等号条件的数据处理不等式，并解决了 Rényi 于 1961 年提出的公开问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shannon、Rényi、Tsallis 等熵及其关联散度广泛用于最大熵推断、最小散度估计与拟合优度检验，但选择何种熵往往依赖惯例而非结构原理。本文旨在建立一个测度论框架，以“绝对连续时输入测度熵不超过参考测度熵”为可容许性公理，从结构上给出选择熵泛函的统一原则，并回答 Rényi 关于哪些广义均值可替代算术均值进入其熵公理化的问题。

Method: 作者引入测度论框架，将可容许性定义为矩不等式（绝对连续蕴含熵序）；在广义均值复合下刻画所有满足该性质的熵泛函，形成由均值生成元、熵尺度和可加性假设依次决定的四级层级。关键判据：连续严格单调的生成元 g 可容许，当且仅当 t↦g(1/t) 在 g 递增时严格凸、在 g 递减时严格凹；该判据被证明等价于相应 Csiszár f-散度生成元的严格凸性，从而通过传递到 f-散度获得马尔可夫核下的数据处理不等式及其精确等号条件。进一步地，乘积可加性单独挑选出 Rényi 族，而内部可加性（或乘积可加性叠加算术均值复合）唯一确定 Shannon 熵。

Result: 得到完整且构造性的熵与散度泛函表征：验证了 Rényi 问题的解答——只有使 g(1/t) 严格凸（g 递增）或严格凹（g 递减）的广义均值可用于其熵公理化；该判据同时导出具有精确等号条件的数据处理不等式；并在层级中定位 Rényi 族与 Shannon 熵的唯一性。此外，构造产生了新的可容许熵族与散度族，包括积分变换型（integral-transform）新家族。

Conclusion: 本文以清晰的测度论公理取代熵选择的惯例化做法，给出可容许熵泛函的完整刻画与四级层级，将 Rényi、Shannon、Tsallis 等熵统一纳入同一结构原理之下，并因其构造性而直接产出新的可容许熵与散度家族，为最大熵推断、最小散度估计等统计方法提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Entropy functionals and their associated divergences underlie many statistical methods, including maximum entropy inference, minimum divergence estimation, and goodness-of-fit testing, yet choosing among Shannon, Rényi, Tsallis, and more general entropies is often a matter of convention rather than structural principle. We introduce a measure theoretic framework in which admissibility requires the entropy of an input measure to be bounded above by that of its reference measure whenever the former is absolutely continuous with respect to the latter. Under generalized mean-value composition, we characterize all such entropy functionals and obtain a four-level hierarchy determined successively by the mean generator, entropy scale, and additivity assumptions. A continuous strictly monotone generator $g$ is admissible exactly when $t\mapsto g(1/t)$ is strictly convex for increasing $g$, or strictly concave for decreasing $g$. This resolves a question posed by Rényi (Proc. 4th Berkeley Sympos. Math. Statist. Prob., 1961) concerning which generalized means may replace the arithmetic mean in his entropy axiomatization. The same criterion is equivalent to strict convexity of an associated Csiszár $f$-divergence generator and therefore yields data processing under Markov kernels with an exact equality condition. Within this hierarchy, product additivity singles out the Rényi family, while internal additivity, or product additivity together with arithmetic mean-value composition, singles out Shannon entropy. The characterization is constructive and yields new admissible entropy and divergence families, including integral-transform examples.

</details>


### [101] [Integrated Information in the Active Inference Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14165)
*Carlotta Langer,Jesse van Oostrum,Nihat Ay*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文将主动推理框架与意识整合信息论（IIT）相结合，在生成模型上定义了一个整合信息度量，并通过模拟智能体实验发现该度量与主动推理智能体的自由能之间存在随模型规模增大而增强的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动推理框架以统一的方式刻画感知与行动选择，为建模有感知（sentient）行为提供了原理性方法；而整合信息论旨在通过系统整合信息的能力来量化意识水平。作者希望将两者结合，从而能够从整合信息的角度分析主动推理智能体及其内在生成模型。

Method: 在主动推理框架中，智能体通过内部生成模型推断未来观测与内部状态并选择动作；作者额外引入一个结构假设，针对该生成模型定义了一个整合信息的度量（借鉴整合信息论的核心思想），随后用模拟智能体进行实验，对比整合信息度量与主动推理中的自由能（变分自由能）之间的关系。

Result: 实验表明，模拟智能体的整合信息度量与主动推理智能体的自由能之间存在相关性，且该相关性随着生成模型规模的增大而增强。

Conclusion: 主动推理智能体的生成模型可以从整合信息的角度进行量化分析，二者之间的联系随模型复杂度提升而更加显著，这为在计算框架内研究意识相关性质提供了可行途径。

Abstract: The active inference framework provides a principled approach to modeling sentient behavior. In this framework perception and action selection are treated in a unified way. The resulting agents form an internal generative model of the relevant dynamics of the world in order to infer their future observations, their internal states and to select actions. We combine this modeling framework with the Integrated Information Theory of consciousness and are therefore able to analyze the active inference agents from the perspective of integrated information. The Integrated Information Theory aims at quantifying the level of consciousness of a system by assessing its capability to integrate information. Here, we define a measure of integrated information for the generative model by making an additional structural assumption. Experiments with simulated agents reveal a correlation between integrated information measures and the free energy of the active inference agents that increases with the size of the generative model.

</details>


### [102] [Positioning with Flexible Reflectors: Solution and Performance Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14184)
*Jiajun He,Han Yu,Danyan Lin,Gaofeng Pan,Hing Cheung So,Stefano Buzzi,Hien Quoc Ngo*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了在视距（LoS）路径不可用的严重阻挡场景下，利用柔性反射器（FRs）群辅助实现目标定位的方案，提出了简单而准确的估计器，推导了克拉美-罗下界（CRLB）并据此优化FR的位置与朝向，同时分析了随机部署下的CRLB分布，仿真证明所提方案接近CRLB性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柔性反射器（FRs）作为一种低成本、高能效的电磁传播环境重塑方案已广泛应用于众多场景，但在LoS路径不可用时，如何借助FR构建的虚拟LoS路径实现精准的目标定位仍是一个关键问题，且缺乏对FR群定位性能极限的系统性分析。

Method: 提出一个利用FRs创建的虚拟LoS路径进行定位的简单且准确的估计器；推导定位方案的CRLB以刻画性能极限，并基于CRLB优化FR的位置与朝向；进一步考虑FR随机部署，刻画CRLB分布并分析不同网络配置对定位精度的影响。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的定位方案性能紧密逼近CRLB；推导的解析结果揭示了FR部署与网络配置对定位精度的影响规律，为FR部署提供了实用指导。

Conclusion: FR群辅助定位在严重阻挡条件下可行且有效，所提估计器接近理论性能下界，所推导的分析结果可作为FR部署与系统设计的实用准则。

Abstract: Flexible reflectors (FRs) have emerged as a low-cost and energy-efficient solution for reshaping electromagnetic propagation environments across a wide range of applications. This paper investigates FR-swarm-assisted target localization in scenarios where line-of-sight (LoS) paths are unavailable. By leveraging the virtual LoS paths created by the FRs, a simple yet accurate estimator is proposed for localization under severe blockage conditions. To characterize the performance limits of the proposed scheme, we derive the Cramer-Rao lower bound (CRLB) and use it to optimize the positions and orientations of the FRs. Furthermore, by accounting for random FR deployment, we characterize the CRLB distribution and reveal how different network configurations affect localization accuracy. Simulation results demonstrate that the developed scheme closely approaches the CRLB performance, while the derived analytical results provide useful guidelines for FR deployment and network design.

</details>


### [103] [Lossy Compression via Sparse Regression Codes: Generalized Construction and Finite-length Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14494)
*Galen Reeves,Ramji Venkataramanan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了稀疏回归码（SPARCs）在简单贪心编码（基于相关性和基于距离）下的有损压缩问题。作者将SPARC构造推广为"加性正交回归码"这一更一般类别，通过追踪各阶段编码残差的演化推导出平方误差失真的非渐近界，并据此优化功率分配以改善有限长度压缩性能，同时为低成本变体（如符号SPARC和K稀疏SPARC）提供失真保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SPARCs在有损压缩中的应用尚需更深入的理论理解，特别是在简单贪心编码规则下的失真行为。现有SPARC构造缺乏统一的推广框架，且功率分配对失真控制的作用未被系统研究。作者旨在为一般化的回归码建立非渐近失真保证，并改进有限长度下的压缩性能，同时覆盖复杂度更低的变体。

Method: 作者将标准SPARC推广为"加性正交回归码"类别，在该类别下分析两类贪心编码规则：基于相关性的编码和基于距离的编码。方法核心是追踪编码残差在各阶段随编码字典列的去除而产生的演化过程（类匹配追踪/正交匹配追踪残差更新），由此推导出平方误差失真的非渐近上界，并利用这些界将失真控制问题转化为功率分配优化问题，根据码参数（如列数、块长度、阶段数）求解最优分配。

Result: 推导出加性正交回归码在有损压缩下的非渐近平方误差失真界；证明功率分配是控制失真的关键因素；基于推导给出的最优功率分配方案改善了SPARCs的有限长度压缩性能；所得失真界同样适用于更低复杂度的变体，包括符号SPARCs（sign-SPARCs）和K稀疏SPARCs。

Conclusion: 加性正交回归码为SPARC推广提供了统一的理论框架；通过优化功率分配可以显著提升有限长度的率失真性能；所建立的失真界为低复杂度SPARC变体提供了理论保证，从而在不牺牲可靠性的前提下实现更高效的实现。

Abstract: We study sparse regression codes (SPARCs) for lossy compression under simple greedy encoding rules, including both correlation-based and distance-based methods. We generalize the SPARC construction, and consider the class of \emph{additive orthogonal} regression codes, of which standard SPARCs are a special case. For this class of codes, we derive nonasymptotic bounds on the squared-error distortion by tracking the evolution of the encoding residual across stages. Our results highlight the role of power allocation in controlling the distortion, allowing us to optimize the allocation based on the parameters of the code. The optimized allocation improves the finite-length compression performance of SPARCs, and our bounds provide distortion guarantees for lower complexity variants of SPARCs, like signed SPARCs and $K$-sparse SPARCs.

</details>


### [104] [Learning-to-Transition for Large-scale and High-Order MIMO Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14511)
*Yubo Zhang,Yiyao Liu,Xiaodong Wang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种学习到转移（L2T）框架，将高维MIMO检测建模为一系列完整向量转移的随机过程。核心方案包括：用通道耦合的Transformer联合更新实例嵌入与采样策略，通过块自回归分解建模流间依赖；硬输出检测采用残差到误码率（residual-to-BER）课程训练；软输出接收则通过"绑定到解绑"（tied-to-untied）的参数级迁移，将硬检测策略克隆到非绑定的软输入软输出迭代检测解码（IDD）接收机每一层，并配合多阶段训练稳定迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维MIMO检测需要在巨大的离散符号空间中高效搜索，同时为信道解码提供可靠的软信息。这两个目标之间存在张力：硬输出强调搜索效率与准确性，软输出则要求生成可靠的似然信息。现有方法难以同时兼顾搜索几何的学习与解码器先验反馈下的软信息生成，亟需一个统一的框架将二者有机衔接。

Method: 1) 将MIMO检测形式化为完整向量转移的随机序列（L2T框架）；2) 每个转移步由通道耦合Transformer更新实例嵌入与采样策略，并用块自回归分解捕捉流间依赖，控制序列复杂度；3) 硬输出：递归应用转移网络，采用"残差→误码率"课程训练，先用精确残差度量学习搜索几何，再对齐发射比特准确率；4) 软输出：将训练好的硬策略在参数层面克隆进非绑定的SISO-IDD接收机每一层（tied-to-untied转移），在解码器反馈下实现逐层、逐轮特化；5) 每个IDD轮内按贝叶斯规则用先验倾斜候选生成，以似然加权的终止假设计算后验与外信息LLR供LDPC解码；6) 多阶段训练策略（合成先验→环路内先验）逐步稳定硬到软迁移。

Result: 通过课程学习与参数级迁移，框架在硬输出上同时获得MIMO搜索几何知识与比特级准确性；在软输出上保留了零先验的搜索动态，同时借助逐层逐轮特化与贝叶斯先验倾斜生成高质量外信息LLR；多阶段训练缓解了硬到软迁移的不稳定性，支撑LDPC迭代解码。

Conclusion: L2T框架统一了MIMO硬输出与软输出检测：硬策略作为软接收机的初始化先验，通过绑定性→非绑定性的参数迁移实现平滑过渡，使接收端在迭代解码反馈下兼顾搜索效率与软信息可靠性。该思路为学习型MIMO检测器与信道解码的闭环协同提供了新范式。

Abstract: High-order multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) detection requires efficient search over a large discrete symbol space while producing reliable soft information for channel decoding. This paper develops a learning-to-transition (L2T) framework that formulates MIMO detection as a stochastic sequence of complete-vector transitions. At each transition, a channel-coupled Transformer updates both the instance embedding and the sampling policy, while a blockwise autoregressive factorization captures inter-stream dependence with moderate sequential complexity. For hard-output detection, a transition network is applied recursively and trained through a residual-to-BER curriculum, which first learns the MIMO search geometry from the exact residual metric and then aligns the policy with transmitted-bit accuracy. For soft-output reception, the well-trained hard policy is cloned at the parameter level into every layer of an untied soft-input soft-output iterative detection and decoding (IDD) receiver. This tied-to-untied transfer preserves the learned zero-prior search dynamics while enabling layer- and round-specific specialization under decoder feedback. Within each IDD round, decoder priors tilt candidate generation according to Bayes' rule, and likelihood-weighted terminal hypotheses produce posterior and extrinsic log-likelihood ratios for LDPC decoding. A multi-stage training strategy further stabilizes the hard-to-soft transfer by progressively exposing the receiver to synthetic and in-loop decoder-generated priors.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [105] [What preferences can - and cannot - predict in multi-agent online learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13810)
*Omar Abbadi,Rida Laraki,Panayotis Mertikopoulos*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究博弈中基于序数偏好的解概念与无遗憾学习动力学（如FTRL）长期行为之间的关系，分析博弈的偏好图（组合数据）在多大程度上决定学习动力学的输出。作者证明动态稳定集的骨架必为偏好稳定集，偏好可在子博弈情形下刻画渐近稳定性，但一般情形下该等价关系不成立（构造三玩家反例），最终借助"关于聚合并发的韧性"这一基于收益的易检验条件弥合差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是探究博弈的组合/序数数据（偏好图）是否以及何时足以决定学习动力学的长期行为——即动态稳定性的判据能否仅由序数偏好刻画，而无需收益的基数信息。作者希望弄清偏好稳定（对有利偏离封闭）与动态稳定（如FTRL等无遗憾动力学的吸引子）之间的等价关系成立与失效的边界。

Method: 首先证明方向一：每个动态稳定集的骨架（所含纯型面）必须偏好稳定（对有利单方偏离封闭）。随后在子博弈（通过限制玩家行动集得到的纯型面子集）情形下证明偏好与渐近稳定性相互刻画。针对一般情形，构造一个三玩家博弈，其中存在偏好稳定集但其张成（span）动态不稳定，以证明等价关系失效；最后引入"关于聚合并发的韧性"这一基于收益的易检验条件，证明它可保证任意纯型面张成的渐近稳定性。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 动态稳定 ⇒ 偏好稳定（骨架层面）；(2) 在子博弈情形，偏好完全刻画渐近稳定性；(3) 一般情形下等价性不成立——给出偏好稳定但张成动态不稳定的三玩家反例；(4) "韧性"（resilience）条件提供了保证任意纯型面张成渐近稳定的充分判据。

Conclusion: 作者总结：序数偏好图在某些受限结构（子博弈）下足以表征学习动力学的稳定性结果，但并不能作为动态稳定性的普适判据；通过引入基于收益、易于检验的"韧性"条件，可弥补这一差距，为纯型面张成的渐近稳定提供充分条件。

Abstract: We examine the interplay between ordinal, preference-based solution concepts in games and the long-run behavior of game dynamics, asking in particular to what extent the combinatorial data of a game -- its preference graph -- determine the outcomes of no-regret learning dynamics -- such as follow-the-regularized-leader (FTRL). In one direction, we show that the skeleton of every dynamically stable set (i.e. the set of pure profiles it contains) must also be preferentially stable, that is, it must be closed under profitable deviations. We then ask the converse question: when do preferences determine the long-run behavior of the players' learning dynamics? We begin by showing that preferences characterize asymptotic stability in the case of subgames -- i.e. subsets of pure profiles obtained by restricting players' action sets. Beyond this case however, the equivalence between dynamic and preferential stability collapses: concretely, we construct a three-player game with a preferentially stable set whose span is dynamically unstable, showing in this way that preferences do not suffice as a criterion of dynamic stability. We then bridge this gap via the notion of resilience under aggregate deviations, an easy-to-check payoff-based condition that guarantees asymptotic stability of arbitrary spans of pure strategies.

</details>


### [106] [Equilibrium Pricing in Oligopolistic Data Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14018)
*Bhaskar Ray Chaudhury,Jugal Garg,Eklavya Sharma,Jiaxin Song*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究寡头数据市场中的均衡定价问题，其中买家（如机器学习公司）预算受限，卖家策略性设定数据集价格。由于数据的非竞争性，传统的竞争均衡保证不再成立：精确纳什均衡（NE）和1.363近似NE在统一定价下甚至可能不存在。作者转向放宽的均衡概念，证明采用分段线性凸定价函数可保证常数因子内的近似稳定性（存在任何卖家偏离统一价格最多将收益提升2倍的定价配置，即针对统一偏离的2-近似NE），并通过仿真展示快速收敛且经验近似保证优于最坏情况界2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据市场的定价问题是机器学习公司向数据卖家购买数据以提升模型精度的核心经济问题。对于竞争性（rivalrous）商品，竞争均衡可保证均衡价格的存在；但由于数据具有非竞争性（non-rivalry），这一保证不复存在。作者希望系统刻画在预算受限买家与策略性卖家构成的寡头数据市场中均衡定价的存在性，并探索能恢复均衡稳定性的替代定价机制。

Method: 作者将卖家竞争定价建模为定价博弈，其纯纳什均衡对应均衡价格。首先证明在统一（uniform）定价下精确NE及1.363-近似NE均可能不存在；随后引入放宽的均衡概念，允许卖家采用超越统一定价的机制——具体为分段线性凸定价函数，证明存在一个定价配置使得任何卖家偏离到任意统一价格最多只能将收益提升两倍（即针对统一偏离的2-近似NE）。最后通过仿真验证收敛速度与经验近似保证。

Result: 主要结果为：(1) 数据非竞争性导致统一定价下精确NE及其1.363-近似NE均可能不存在，与竞争性商品的竞争均衡保证形成鲜明对比；(2) 采用分段线性凸定价函数可保证常数因子2内的近似稳定性（2-近似NE，针对统一偏离）；(3) 仿真表明定价收敛快速，且经验近似保证优于最坏情况的理论界2。

Conclusion: 研究表明，数据市场的非竞争性从根本上改变了均衡定价的性质，简单的统一定价机制无法保证均衡存在；而更灵活的分段线性凸定价函数可以在常数因子内恢复近似均衡稳定性，为实际数据市场的定价机制设计提供了理论依据，且实际表现优于最坏情况保证。

Abstract: We study equilibrium pricing in oligopolistic data markets with budget-constrained buyers (e.g., machine learning companies purchasing data to improve model accuracy) and strategic data sellers. Sellers compete by setting prices for their datasets, giving rise to a pricing game whose pure Nash equilibria correspond to equilibrium prices. While equilibrium prices are guaranteed for rivalrous goods via competitive equilibrium, we show that the non-rivalry of data fundamentally alters this picture: an exact Nash equilibrium (NE) need not exist, and in fact, 1.363-approximate NE may also not exist under uniform pricing. We therefore investigate relaxed equilibrium notions. Allowing sellers to use beyond-uniform pricing---specifically, piecewise-linear convex pricing functions---guarantees approximate stability within a constant factor: there exists a pricing profile in which no seller can improve revenue by a factor of two by deviating to any uniform price (a 2-approximate NE against uniform deviations). Finally, our simulations demonstrate fast convergence and empirical approximation guarantees that outperform the worst-case bound of 2.

</details>


### [107] [Optimal Pricing and Charging Strategy Design for Non-cooperative Battery Swapping Stations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14167)
*Huanyu Yan,Chenxi Sun,Huanxin Liao,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文针对由同一聚合商协调的多个竞争性电池换电站（BSS），提出了一种两层博弈模型：第一阶段BSS在日前市场确定换电价格，第二阶段在实时市场确定电池充电策略，并严格证明了子博弈完美纳什均衡（SPNE）的存在性与唯一性，进而提出最优定价与充电策略及预测误差处理方法，在西安12个BSS的真实数据仿真中将单个BSS利润提升至少18.1%，并天然实现削峰填谷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电池换电是电动汽车快速补能的重要方式，随着越来越多主体参与建设BSS，非合作BSS如何在竞争市场中最大化自身利润成为亟待研究的问题；现有研究缺乏对多个竞争性BSS在统一聚合商协调下的定价与充电协同优化的深入分析。

Method: 构建层级博弈（Stackelberg式）模型：第一阶段在日前市场中由BSS制定换电价格，第二阶段在实时市场中优化电池充电策略；严格证明SPNE的存在性与唯一性，并基于唯一均衡提出各BSS的最优定价与充电策略，另设计预测误差处理方法以应对换电需求的意外波动。

Result: 基于西安真实数据的12-BSS系统仿真表明，所提定价与充电策略可使单个BSS利润至少提升18.1%，且最优充电策略天然实现了电网削峰填谷效果。

Conclusion: SPNE的唯一性为竞争环境下均衡策略的最优性提供了理论支撑；所提策略能显著提升竞争市场中各BSS的利润，兼具需求波动鲁棒性与电网负荷调节效益。

Abstract: Battery swapping is a rapid way to recharge electric vehicles (EVs). As more and more entities are involved in building Battery Swapping Stations (BSSs), how non-cooperative BSSs maximize their profit in a competitive market needs further investigation. In this paper, we focus on a practical scenario where competitive BSSs are coordinated by the same aggregator. To study the optimal pricing and battery charging, we formulate a hierarchical game-theoretic model, where BSSs determine the swapping price in the day-ahead market in the first stage, and then determine the optimal battery charging strategy in the real-time market in the second stage. We rigorously prove the existence and uniqueness of the Subgame Perfect Nash Equilibrium (SPNE). In particular, the uniqueness property provides theoretical support that the strategy under equilibrium is optimal in the competitive environment. Based on the unique SPNE, we propose an optimal pricing and charging strategy for each BSS to maximize profit in the competitive market. A prediction error handling method is also proposed to deal with unexpected fluctuations in swapping demand. Our simulation with a 12-BSS system based on real-life data from Xi'an, China shows that our pricing and charging strategy increases the individual BSS profit by at least 18.1\%, while the optimal charging strategy naturally achieves peak shaving for the power grid.

</details>


### [108] [Individual Rationality in Constrained Hedonic Games: Friends, Enemies, and Neutrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14461)
*Šimon Schierrreich,Ildikó Schlotter*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究由朋友、敌人和中性关系诱导的受限联盟形成（hedonic game），在朋友导向与敌人导向两类可加可分偏好下，寻找满足个体理性（IR）、恰好 k 个非空联盟且每个联盟规模受上下界约束的划分。虽然仅要求 IR 很平凡，但加入规模约束后可行性判定变得计算困难；敌人导向情形可归结为带规模约束的图着色，朋友导向情形则更复杂，其难度由敌意结构、规模约束的严格程度以及关系对称性共同决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在彻底刻画在带规模约束的现实联盟形成问题中，朋友导向与敌人导向两种偏好模型的计算复杂性边界。由于仅 IR 约束平凡可满足，需引入恰好 k 个联盟及规模上下界这些实际约束后，判定可行划分的存在性，从而理解何种结构性质决定问题的难易。

Method: 将问题形式化为带下界/上界且恰好 k 个联盟的 IR 划分判定问题；对敌人导向偏好将其归约为规模约束图着色问题，对朋友导向偏好则依据敌意结构、规模要求严格程度以及关系是否对称等因素，进行经典复杂度与参数化复杂度两方面的系统性分析与归约。

Result: 敌人导向下问题复杂度完全随规模约束图着色而定；朋友导向下问题复杂度由敌意结构主导，且受两个因素影响：规模要求的严格程度、关系对称性。多个情形在对称关系中可解，但允许非对称后变为不可解，作者给出了经典复杂度与参数化复杂度两方面的完整边界刻画。

Conclusion: 论文揭示了两种偏好模型在带规模约束的 IR 联盟划分问题上存在显著差异，并完整刻画了朋友/敌意结构性质与复杂度之间对应的边界，为该类受限联盟形成问题提供了系统的复杂性全景。

Abstract: We study constrained coalition formation in games induced by friends, enemies, and neutrals, under the two standard refinements of additively separable preferences: friend-oriented and enemy-oriented. We ask for partitions that are individually rational (IR), while additionally requiring exactly $k$ non-empty coalitions, each satisfying a prescribed lower and upper bound on its size. Although IR alone is trivial to satisfy for any hedonic game, the size constraints make it computationally intractable to decide whether a feasible partition exists.
  The two models tell strikingly different stories. Under enemy-oriented preferences, the problem collapses to size-constrained graph coloring, and its complexity follows accordingly. Under friend-oriented preferences, however, the picture is far more intricate, and is governed by the enmity structure rather than the friendships. The complexity is further shaped by two factors: how strict the imposed size requirements are, and whether relationships are symmetric or asymmetric, with several cases turning out tractable in the symmetric setting but intractable once asymmetry is allowed. Charting this boundary in terms of both classical and parameterized complexity, we provide a complete understanding of which properties of the friend/enemy structure are responsible for hardness.

</details>


### [109] [Ex-ante versus Ex-post: Egalitarian Facility Location Mechanism Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14499)
*Zohar Barak,Inbal Talgam-Cohen*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究欧氏空间中单设施选址的机制设计问题，关注在策略性（期望意义下防策略操纵）约束下最小化最大代理成本（平等主义成本）。低维下证明了“事前”（ex-ante）与“事后”（ex-post）评价的严格分离，给出R上一比一近似机制和R²上近似比≤1.598的随机机制；高维下则证明了不可能性结果，说明随机机制无法显著超越确定性独裁机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定性机制的平等主义近似比最优为2（由独裁机制达到）。作者希望探索随机化策略性期望机制能否改善公平性，并指出已有工作仅关注“事后”评价（期望最大成本），而“事前”评价（最大期望成本）与期望策略性自然地契合，故需系统性研究事前与事后两种目标下的能力边界。

Method: 在R中构造简单策略性机制达到事前近似比1；在R²中设计“Random Rotated Corner”（随机旋转角落）机制，其事前近似比不超过1.598；对事后目标证明下界1.605，从而在R²中建立严格分离。高维情形通过下界证明说明没有任何期望策略性机制能在o_d(1)意义上改进确定性独裁机制，覆盖事前与事后两种评价，并分析了RRCWM机制在高维平等主义目标下的最优性。

Result: (1) 低维：R中存在事前近似比为1的策略性机制（最优）；R²中“Random Rotated Corner”机制达到≤1.598的事前近似比，突破确定性2的壁垒；而事后目标下界为1.605，表明R²中事前与事后评价存在严格分离。(2) 高维：对d≫1，不存在能在o_d(1)意义下优于确定性独裁机制的期望策略性机制，因此事前、事后评价都无法在高维带来公平性改善；RRCWM机制在所有d≥1下对两种目标近似比均为2，且为最佳。

Conclusion: 随机机制的能力高度依赖于维度和评价标准：在低维尤其是R²中，“事前”评价能突破确定性壁垒并优于“事后”评价；而在高维中，随机化与评价方式的选择都无法改善平等主义公平性，确定性独裁机制在渐近意义下已是最优。RRCWM在高维平等主义目标上也是最优的。

Abstract: We study the facility location mechanism design problem where $n$ strategic agents report locations in Euclidean space and the mechanism outputs a single facility location. Each agent's cost is its distance from the facility, and our objective is to minimize the egalitarian cost, i.e., the maximum agent cost, in a strategyproof way.
  The optimal deterministic approximation ratio is $2$, achieved by any dictator mechanism. We study the power of randomized strategyproof-in-expectation mechanisms. Prior work has focused on ex-post evaluation, defined as the expected maximum agent cost. We instead study ex-ante evaluation, defined as the maximum expected agent cost, which is naturally aligned with strategyproofness in expectation.
  We establish the following results:
  (1) Low dimensions: Strict ex-ante vs. ex-post separation. In $\mathbb{R}$, we give a simple strategyproof mechanism achieving the optimal ex-ante approximation ratio of $1$. In $\mathbb{R}^2$, we design the "Random Rotated Corner" mechanism, with ex-ante approximation ratio at most $1.598$, breaking the deterministic barrier. For the ex-post objective, we prove a lower bound of $1.605$, yielding a strict separation in $\mathbb{R}^2$.
  (2) High dimensions: Impossibility. In $\mathbb{R}^d$ for $d \gg 1$, we show that no strategyproof-in-expectation mechanism improves on the deterministic dictator mechanism beyond $o_d(1)$. Thus neither ex-post nor ex-ante evaluation yields improved fairness guarantees in high dimensions. An implication is that the "Random Rotation Coordinate-Wise Median" (RRCWM), currently the best known mechanism for the utilitarian objective, is also best possible for the egalitarian objective in high dimension: we show it achieves an approximation ratio of $2$ for both ex-post and ex-ante objectives in $\mathbb{R}^d$ for every $d \ge 1$.

</details>


### [110] [Forging Self-Funded Marketplaces among Strategic Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14548)
*Yuan Deng,Vasilis Gkatzelis,Xizhi Tan,Grigoris Velegkas,Song Zuo*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究自筹资金市场（self-funded marketplaces）的机制设计问题：策略性代理付出努力并产生收入与成本，机制需在保证个体理性（补偿成本）和预算平衡（总支付不超过总收入）的前提下最大化总价值。该问题推广了经典预算可行（budget-feasible）机制设计。作者证明（1）针对最优可行基准（first-best），任何真实拍卖都无法实现有界近似比；（2）即使在受限设定下也无法优于对数近似，并提出一类序贯拍卖通过子博弈完美均衡达到对数近似；（3）引入更能刻画市场厚度的最大份额（MMS）基准，并设计一个序贯拍卖使得子博弈完美均衡达到常数近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机制设计主要关注预算外生给定的预算可行问题，但实际中市场（如众包平台、去中心化市场）通常需要由参与者的自身收入自筹资金。本文动机在于设计能够激励策略性代理投入努力、同时满足成本补偿与自筹预算平衡约束的机制，并研究能达到何种近似保证。

Method: 作者首先以无可信信息的最优可行解（first-best）为基准，通过下界论证说明真实（truthful）拍卖无法获得有界近似比，且在受限设置下不存在优于对数近似的拍卖。随后提出一类序贯（sequential）拍卖，利用子博弈完美均衡作为解概念来保证对数近似。为克服first-best过严的限制，进一步引入最大化份额（MMS）作为备选基准以刻画市场"厚度"，并设计其均衡达到常数近似的拍卖机制。

Result: （1）不存在可对first-best基准达到有界近似的真实拍卖；（2）即使在受限设定下，任何拍卖的近似比都不能优于对数；（3）所提序贯拍卖在子博弈完美均衡下达到对数近似；（4）对MMS基准，所设计的序贯拍卖在子博弈完美均衡下达到常数近似。

Conclusion: 结果表明自筹资金市场的机制设计比传统预算可行问题更具挑战性：以first-best为基准过于严苛，只能达到对数近似且无法由真实机制实现；而以MMS为基准则能够更好地反映市场厚度，并获得常数近似。这为设计自筹资金市场的激励兼容机制提供了理论可行性与近似保证层面的指导。

Abstract: We introduce the problem of designing mechanisms that incentivize strategic agents to form self-funded marketplaces. In our model, if agent $i$ exerts effort $x_i\in [0,1]$, they incur a cost of $x_i\cdot c_i$ (where $c_i$ is unknown to the mechanism designer) and they generate revenue $x_i\cdot r_i$; crucially, $c_i$ can be greater or smaller than $r_i$. Each effort profile $\mathbf{x}$ yields value $v(\mathbf{x})$ and the objective is to choose an effort vector that maximizes the value while ensuring that every agent $i$ receives a payment $p_i\geq x_i\cdot c_i$ and that $\mathbf{x}$ is budget-balanced, i.e., $\sum_{i} p_i \leq \sum_{i} x_i\cdot r_i$. This problem generalizes the well-studied budget-feasible mechanism design problem, where the requirement is that $\sum_{i} p_i \leq B$ for some predetermined budget $B$.
  To evaluate the performance of such mechanisms, we first consider the first-best benchmark (the optimal value achievable in the absence of any private information) and show that no truthful auction can achieve a bounded approximation of this benchmark. Also, even in restricted settings, no auction can achieve better than a logarithmic approximation. We complement these results by proposing a class of sequential auctions whose subgame perfect equilibria guarantee a logarithmic approximation of this benchmark. We then introduce an alternative benchmark, the maximin share (MMS), that better captures the thickness of the market and we provide an auction whose subgame perfect equilibria achieve a constant approximation of this benchmark.

</details>

### [111] [Strand-based Hairstyle Generation via Large Reconstruction and Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13679)
*Conghui Hao,Tao Huang,Yuefan Shen,Tongtong Wang,Zhongtian Zheng,Kui Wu*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需任务特定训练或数据采集的自动管线，结合大重建模型（LRM）、大多模态模型（LMM）与传统几何处理，从单视角图像生成高质量、可直接用于生产的发丝级（strand-based）发型几何，可处理直发、卷发、长短发以及发髻、马尾等复杂结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生产管线中创建高质量发丝级发型高度依赖熟练艺术家与耗时的手工建模，成本高且难以规模化。已有的基于学习的方法虽推动了图像驱动的头发重建，但通常需要大量多样的训练数据、难以泛化到发髻和马尾等复杂样式，且其表示往往无法与基于发丝建模、编辑和仿真直接兼容。

Method: 提出一个全新自动管线，将大重建模型（LRMs）、大多模态模型（LMMs）与经典几何处理相结合：利用LRM/LMM进行图像理解与初始重建，再借助经典几何处理生成发丝级几何。该方法不需要任务特定的训练或数据收集。

Result: 在多样化的发型（包括直发、卷发、短发、长发、马尾和发髻等结构化配置）上均能生成视觉上令人信服的发丝级重建，且仅需几分钟即可完成，适合集成到现代生产管线中。

Conclusion: 该方法在无需专用训练数据的前提下，实现了高效、可扩展到多种复杂发型的高质量发丝重建，填补了现有学习方法的泛化性与表示兼容性缺口，为数字人/角色生产流程中的发型创作提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Creating high-quality strand-based hairstyles in current production pipelines remains heavily dependent on skilled artists and time-consuming manual authoring, making it costly and difficult to scale. Existing learning-based methods have advanced image-driven hair reconstruction, but typically require large, diverse training datasets, struggle to generalize to complex styles such as buns and ponytails, and often operate in representations that are not directly compatible with strand-based modeling, editing, and simulation. We present a novel automatic pipeline that combines the capabilities of Large Reconstruction Models (LRMs), Large Multimodal Models (LMMs), and classical geometry processing to generate high-quality strand-based hairstyles from single-view images. Our approach produces detailed, production-ready strand geometry without task-specific training or data collection and can handle a wide variety of hairstyles, including straight and curly hair, short and long styles, and challenging structured configurations such as ponytails and buns. Across this diverse set of examples, our method generates visually compelling strand-level reconstructions within only a few minutes, making it well-suited for integration into modern digital human workflows.

</details>


### [112] [Uncertainty-Aware Jacobi Set Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14409)
*Daniel Klötzl,Daniel Weiskopf*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向不确定性的Jacobi集计算方法，通过将已有的基于边识别、适用于三角网格的Jacobi集计算框架推广到具有空间相干性的多元正态分布不确定场中，实现解析的不确定性传播，并设计了叠加可视化映射以同时展示Jacobi集及其多维不确定性信息；该方法在解析数据集上对照蒙特卡洛方法验证，并应用于流体动力学与天气集合预报两个案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jacobi集是刻画两个标量场梯度对齐关系的拓扑描述子，常用于多场可视化；但在真实数据中普遍存在测量噪声与模拟不确定性，而现有Jacobi集计算并未显式考虑输入场的不确定性，难以满足科学可视化对可靠性的需求。因此需要一种能够感知并传播输入不确定性的Jacobi集计算方法，并配套相应的不确定性可视化映射。

Method: 首先采用并重新表述已有的基于三角剖分上边识别Jacobi集边的计算方法；其次提出一种基于多元正态分布、具有空间相干性且适用于标量场对的通用不确定场模型；接着在该模型下解析地传播不确定性，从而将原始Jacob集计算推广到不确定输入；最后设计叠加的可视化映射方案，专门用于在展示Jacobi集的同时呈现不确定性的不同侧面。

Result: 在解析数据集上，所提出的不确定性模型与不确定性感知方法均通过了与蒙特卡洛方法的对照验证，并成功应用于流体动力学和天气集合两个实际用例，表明该方法能够有效刻画并可视化不确定输入下的Jacobi集及其不确定性。

Conclusion: 该研究将Jacobi集这一多场拓扑描述子从确定性输入拓展至不确定输入，提供了解析的不确定性传播框架与配套可视化方案，为多场数据的可信分析与可视化提供了新的技术手段。

Abstract: We present an uncertainty-aware Jacobi set computation method. In general, Jacobi sets are topological descriptors that capture the gradient alignments of two scalar fields, as, e.g., used for multi-field visualization. We adopt and reformulate an existing computational approach that relies on an edge-based identification of Jacobi set edges on a given triangulation. Our extension to uncertainty visualization builds upon a versatile, spatially coherent uncertainty model for pairs of scalar fields based on multivariate normal distributions. We propagate the uncertainty analytically, thereby lifting the original Jacobi set computation to uncertain inputs. Furthermore, we present an overlay of visual mappings specifically designed to show the Jacobi sets along with different facets of uncertainty information. Both the uncertainty model and uncertainty-aware method are validated against a Monte Carlo approach on an analytic dataset and applied to two use cases from fluid dynamics and weather ensembles.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [113] [Inducing Reward-Free Judging Rubrics that Reduce Over-Crediting in Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13564)
*Darragh Quinn,David Dylan,Roisin Healy,Fionn Carroll,Maeve Donnelly,Cormac Sheehan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 RubricForge，一个从少量带真实标签（环境奖励）的轨迹中通过反思式进化自动归纳评估细则的方法，生成人类可读的评分文本，并冻结后在无环境访问情况下以单次模型调用评估未见轨迹。其核心贡献在于提升“忠实性”（降低假通过率）而非原始一致率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模评估语言模型智能体日益依赖另一个语言模型作为自动裁判（因为环境奖励这一金标准在部署时昂贵、缓慢或不可用）。这类裁判是无奖励的代理评估器，其价值取决于是否可信，但现有裁判要么手写评分细则（如 G-Eval），要么微调裁判权重，二者都倾向于给流畅但不成功的轨迹打分过高。

Method: RubricForge 从一小批带真实标签（ground-truth labeled）的轨迹中归纳裁判细则文本，通过“反思式进化”（reflective evolution）不断迭代细则以最大化与环境奖励的一致性，然后冻结该细则，在无环境访问的情况下用单次模型调用对留出轨迹进行评判。优化产物为人类可读文本，每个判决都可归因到具名标准。

Result: 使用同一个冻结的 7B 模型同时作为智能体和裁判，在 tau-bench（220 次 rollout 中取 173 条带标签轨迹）和 WebShop（160 条）上实验：主要收益是忠实性而非原始一致率。与通用 G-Eval 裁判相比差异在统计上不显著（McNemar p=0.248），绝对分数校准上通用裁判略优（|err| 差 -0.048，p=2×10^-4）；但 RubricForge 对失败轨迹的过度表扬约减半（tau-bench 假通过率 0.115 vs 0.173，捕捉到三例过度表扬且零反转），并在 WebShop 的分级结果排序上更忠实（Spearman 0.410 vs 0.370）。

Conclusion: 对无奖励的评估器而言，假通过率（而非总体一致率）才是部署相关指标：假通过会让有缺陷的智能体上线，而假失败只是多花一次重试的代价。因此 RubricForge 通过真实结果锚定的可读细则，在部署关键维度上更具可信度。

Abstract: Evaluating language-model agents at scale increasingly relies on a second language model as an automatic judge, because the gold signal, an executable environment reward, is expensive, slow, or unavailable at deployment time. Such a judge is a reward-free proxy whose value depends on whether it can be trusted, yet existing judges either hand-write the scoring rubric, as in G-Eval, or fine-tune the judge's weights, and both tend to credit fluent but unsuccessful trajectories as successes. We instead induce the text of an agent-judging rubric from a small set of ground-truth-labeled trajectories, grounding it in true outcomes. We present RubricForge, which evolves a judge rubric by reflective evolution against labeled trajectories to maximize agreement with the environment reward, freezes it, and applies it to held-out trajectories in one model call with no environment access. The optimized artifact is human-readable text, so every verdict is attributable to named criteria. Using one frozen 7B model as both agent and judge, on tau-bench (173 labeled trajectories drawn from 220 rollouts) and WebShop (160), the principal gain is faithfulness rather than raw agreement. The edge over a generic G-Eval judge is not statistically significant (McNemar p = 0.248), and absolute-score calibration marginally favors the generic judge (|err| difference -0.048, p = 2x10^-4). Yet RubricForge over-credits failed trajectories roughly half as often (0.115 vs. 0.173 false-pass rate on tau-bench, with three over-credit catches and zero reversals) and ranks graded WebShop outcomes more faithfully (Spearman 0.410 vs. 0.370). For a reward-free evaluator the false-pass rate, not aggregate agreement, is the deployment-relevant quantity, since a false pass ships a broken agent whereas a false fail merely costs a retry.

</details>


### [114] [Depth-Aware Sensitivity Analysis of Mixture-of-Experts Models via Magnitude-Based Expert Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13565)
*Pradeep Kumar Sharma,Shantanu Godbole,Hritvik Shrivastava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对Qwen3.6-35B-A3B（40个MoE层、每层256个专家、top-8路由）进行了系统的逐层敏感性分析，在XLCoST跨语言代码翻译基准上通过幅度掩蔽评估模型压缩。核心发现是层敏感性具有强深度依赖：早期层（0-9）与中层（10-29）对专家掩蔽高度脆弱，而晚期层（30-39）尤其极晚期层（35-39）能容忍对低幅度专家的大规模掩蔽。晚期聚焦策略在掩蔽640-1,145个专家时保留249-255/300的Good+Similar输出，而平坦的全层30%掩蔽仅保留150/300；在500条样本的验证切片上，极晚期策略（35-39层@50%）以仅掩蔽640/10,240个专家保留419/500，取得最佳质量/掩蔽专家数权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构通过稀疏激活在保持计算效率的同时扩展LLM规模，已被广泛采用，但各MoE层的相对重要性尚未被充分刻画，尤其在模型压缩场景下缺乏系统的逐层敏感性认识。

Method: 采用基于幅度的专家掩蔽（magnitude-based expert masking）对Qwen3.6-35B-A3B（40个MoE层、每层256个专家、top-8路由）进行逐层敏感性分析；在XLCoST跨语言代码翻译基准上开展多阶段实验，分别使用100、300、500条提示的评估规模，并在三台H100 GPU服务器上进行；此外还测试了将top-k路由宽度从8降至6个活跃专家，并与专家掩蔽组合观察。

Result: 层敏感性呈强深度依赖：早期层（0-9）与中层（10-29）对专家掩蔽高度脆弱，晚期层（30-39）尤其是极晚期层（35-39）能容忍对低幅度专家的激进掩蔽。平坦全层30%掩蔽在300条提示下仅保留150/300的Good+Similar输出，而晚期聚焦策略保留249-255/300并掩蔽640-1,145个专家。在500条提示的留出验证切片上，极晚期策略（35-39层@50%）保留419/500并仅掩蔽640/10,240个专家，取得最佳质量/掩蔽数权衡。将top-k从8降至6在100条提示探针上显著降低墙钟时间且无Good+Similar损失，但与激进专家掩蔽组合尚不完美。

Conclusion: 这些发现为深度感知（depth-aware）的MoE专家掩蔽提供了经验基础，并确立了通往物理权重手术、基于激活的专家评分以及基于激活的专家评分等实用路径。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures scale large language models (LLMs) while preserving computational efficiency through sparse activation. Despite their widespread adoption, the relative importance of individual MoE layers remains insufficiently characterized, particularly for model compression. This paper presents a systematic layer-wise sensitivity analysis of the Qwen3.6-35B-A3B model (40 MoE layers, 256 experts per layer, top-8 routing) using magnitude-based expert masking on the XLCoST cross-lingual code translation benchmark. We conduct a multi-phase study spanning 100, 300, and 500 prompt evaluation scales across three H100 GPU servers. Our central finding is that layer sensitivity is strongly depth-dependent: early layers (0-9) and middle layers (10-29) are highly fragile to expert masking, while late layers (30-39), and especially very-late layers (35-39), tolerate aggressive masking of low-magnitude experts. Flat all-layer masking at 30% retains only 150/300 Good+Similar outputs at 300-prompt scale, whereas late-focused policies retain 249-255/300 while masking 640-1,145 experts. On a later 500-prompt held-out validation slice, the narrow very-late policy (layers 35-39 @ 50%) achieves the strongest quality/masked-expert tradeoff among tested candidates, retaining 419/500 Good+Similar outputs while masking only 640 of 10,240 total experts. We additionally characterize top-k routing width reduction from 8 to 6 active experts per token, which shows a large observed wall-clock reduction on a 100-prompt probe with no Good+Similar loss, though it does not yet compose cleanly with aggressive expert masking. These findings provide an empirical foundation for depth-aware MoE expert masking and establish a practical path toward physical weight surgery, activation-based expert scoring, and training-based recovery.

</details>


### [115] [Benchmarking data-driven material models on the classic Treloar dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14063)
*Hagen Holthusen,Moritz Flaschel,Denisa Martonová,Ellen Kuhl*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文以 Treloar 经典实验数据为基准，系统比较了六种机器学习驱动的超弹性本构建模框架（广义不变量本构人工神经网络、物理增强神经网络、（自适应）材料指纹识别以及高效无监督本构律识别与发现），从拟合性能、计算成本、超参数敏感性与实现难易度等维度评估，并公开了全部源代码与数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习正在快速重塑本构建模领域，能够直接从实验数据学习材料行为并挑战传统建模范式。然而随着基于机器学习的方法日益增多，实践中如何选择合适的方法缺乏系统的横向对比依据。

Method: 以 Treloar 的经典实验数据为统一基准，对六种流行超弹性建模框架进行评测，比较其拟合性能、计算成本、超参数敏感性与实现难易度；同时通过量化发现模型中的材料参数数量以及评估本构模型及其导数所需的计算时间来刻画预测精度与模型复杂度之间的权衡。

Result: 所有方法均能出色地复现基准数据。研究未选出单一最优方法，而是揭示了每种方法各自的优势与局限，指出结果对实现细节与超参数选择的高度依赖。

Conclusion: 研究为实践者提供了针对不同方法使用场景的实用指导；六种方法的全部源代码、训练与比较脚本、结果与数据均已通过 https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.21915635 公开，以促进可复现性与后续研究。

Abstract: Machine learning is rapidly reshaping constitutive modeling, offers new ways to learn material behavior directly from experimental data, and challenges long-established modeling paradigms. But with a growing number of machine-learning-based approaches available, how do they compare in practice? In this paper, we use the classic experimental data of Treloar to benchmark popular frameworks for hyperelasticity: (Generalized-Invariant) Constitutive Artificial Neural Networks, Physics-Augmented Neural Networks, (Adaptive) Material Fingerprinting, and Efficient Unsupervised Constitutive Law Identification & Discovery. We compare their fitting performance, computational cost, hyperparameter sensitivity, and ease of implementation. Furthermore, we discuss the trade-offs between predictive accuracy and model complexity. The latter is assessed by quantifying both the number of material parameters in the discovered models and the computational time required to evaluate the constitutive model and its derivatives. The results show that all methods can reproduce the benchmark data remarkably well. Rather than identifying a single winner, we highlight the strengths and limitations of each approach and provide practical guidance for their use. The source code for all six methods, including the training and comparison scripts, as well as all results and data used in this study, is publicly available via https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.21915635.

</details>


### [116] [Modular Cognitive Architecture Emerges in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13567)
*Pengrui Han,Jacob Andreas,Evelina Fedorenko,Andrea Gregor de Varda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过电路分析在46个任务、四大认知领域（语言、形式推理、社会推理、物理推理）中考察大语言模型（LLM）的神经组织，发现LLM呈现出与人类大脑相似的功能模块化架构：在人类中使用相同网络的任务会招募LLM中重叠的神经元，而使用不同网络的任务则招募不同神经元，提示模块化可能是智能系统的基本属性而非生物大脑的进化偶然。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类大脑展现出显著的功能特化，不同网络分别支撑语言、形式推理、心理理论推理和物理世界推理。作者想探究这种模块化组织究竟是智能系统构建的根本原则，还是仅属于生物大脑的进化偶然产物，因此选择由完全不同优化过程产生的另一类智能系统——大语言模型——作为检验对象。

Method: 对涵盖四个认知领域（语言、形式推理、社会推理、物理推理）的N=46个任务进行电路分析，比较任务与人类不同神经网络的对应关系，以及LLM内部神经元的重叠/分离模式，检验跨系统中功能模块的映射一致性。

Result: 研究发现LLM确实发展出与人类大脑镜像对应的模块化架构：同一人类网络所对应的任务在LLM中招募相互重叠的神经元，而不同网络对应的任务则招募相互分离的神经元，即LLM的内部组织与人类大脑的功能网络拓扑存在一致对应。

Conclusion: 模块化在大脑与神经网络中收敛式地涌现，表明它可能是智能系统的一种基本性质（fundamental property），而非生物进化路径的偶然副产品，这为理解通用智能系统的组织原理提供了跨物种、跨范式的证据。

Abstract: The human brain exhibits a striking degree of functional specialization, with distinct networks supporting language, formal reasoning, reasoning about other minds, and reasoning about the physical world. Is this modular organization a fundamental principle of how intelligent systems must be built, or an evolutionary accident specific to biological brains? Here, we test whether a similar organization emerges in Large Language Models--another class of intelligent systems created through a very different optimization process. Using circuit analyses across N=46 tasks spanning four cognitive domains (language, formal reasoning, social reasoning, physical reasoning), we find that LLMs develop a modular architecture that mirrors the human brain: tasks drawing on the same network in humans recruit overlapping neurons in LLMs, whereas tasks drawing on different networks recruit distinct neurons. The convergent emergence of modularity in brains and neural networks suggests that it may be a fundamental property of intelligent systems.

</details>


### [117] [A Year in LLM Serving: Workload Evolution, Caching and Load-Balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13573)
*William Nixon,Jon Durbin,Florian Standhartinger,Haryadi S. Gunawi,Juncheng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过对 Chutes 平台为期一年的生产环境 LLM 服务 trace 进行全球特征刻画与纵向研究，从聚合、时间、模型和用户四个层面分析工作负载，揭示生产流量中隐藏的演化规律与用户-模型交互结构，并将发布完整的一年 trace 以支持后续研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 服务已成为关键云工作负载，但现有工作负载研究在规模和范围上受限，通常只观察较短时间段，且对生产环境中用户与模型的交互方式可见性不足，无法充分刻画工作负载随时间的演化以及用户-模型交互对生产流量的影响。

Method: 基于 Chutes 平台捕捉的覆盖多模型、多用户（包括热门与长尾模型）的完整一年生产 trace，从聚合、时间、模型级和用户级四个视角进行全局特征刻画与纵向分析，以揭示通常被聚合视图掩盖的工作负载演化与用户-模型结构。

Result: 本文揭示了 LLM 服务工作负载的演化趋势以及用户与模型之间的交互结构，这些特征在以往研究的聚合视角下难以显现；同时将随论文发布完整的一年 trace，供未来生产行为研究使用，无需依赖采样或合成数据。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了 LLM 服务负载研究在时间跨度与覆盖范围上的空白，提供了更全面的生产负载理解，并通过公开完整 trace 为下游系统评测与建模研究奠定数据基础。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) serving has become a critical cloud workload, and realistic traces are essential for motivating and benchmarking serving systems. However, existing LLM serving workload studies remain limited in scale and scope. They often observe short time periods and provide limited visibility into how users interact with models in production. As a result, they do not fully capture how LLM serving workloads evolve over time or how user-model interactions shape production traffic.
  In this work, we further the understanding of real-world LLM serving workloads through both a global characterization and a longitudinal study of a one-year production trace from Chutes. Unlike prior studies, our trace captures full production behavior across many models and users, including both popular and long-tail models. We analyze the workload from aggregate, temporal, model-level, and user-level perspectives, revealing workload evolution and user-model structure that are typically hidden behind aggregate views. To support future research, we will release the full one-year trace with the paper, enabling downstream studies of production behavior without relying on sampled or synthetically generated workloads.

</details>


### [118] [Agentao: A Governed Local-First Runtime for Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13574)
*Bo Jin,Qiang Jiao,Xin Tong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Agentao，一个面向工具使用型 LLM 智能体的受治理本地优先运行时。其核心思想是将模型生成的动作提案与宿主机授权的执行相互分离，通过分层架构（宿主交互面、宿主契约、运行时核心、基于权限的工具系统及记忆/重放/插件/技能/子代理/协议集成等支撑子系统）把权限、状态、协议边界和执行轨迹显式化为运行时抽象，从而提升智能体的可治理性、可检查性，使其更适合宿主受控的本地环境。代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体正越来越多地作为执行系统运作，会调用工具、修改本地状态、使用持久记忆并参与外部协议交互。这些能力带来实用性的同时，也引入了过度权限动作、审计性薄弱、提示注入、工具投毒及不受控副作用等风险。现有系统缺少对这类执行过程的结构化治理机制，需要一种能将授权、权限与可观测轨迹作为一等运行时抽象来设计的方案。

Method: Agentao 采用"模型生成动作提案、宿主授权执行"的分离式设计，构建分层架构：宿主面向的表层接口、宿主契约、运行时核心、权限中介的工具系统，以及用于记忆、重放、插件、技能、子代理与协议集成等支撑子系统。文中阐述了动机、威胁模型、设计目标、治理模型、执行流水线与结构化事件接口，并以开源代码形式提供实现。

Result: Agentao 演示了如何将权限、状态、协议边界与执行轨迹构建为显式的运行时抽象，从而支撑更加可治理、可检查、适合宿主受控本地环境的智能体构建。作者明确指出该系统不提供形式化安全保障，其价值在于治理机制及可观测性的工程实践。

Conclusion: Agentao 表明通过将治理机制内嵌为运行时的一等抽象，可以在不声称形式化安全保证的前提下显著改善 LLM 智能体的可治理性与可审计性。该工作的意义在于为宿主受控的本地智能体执行提供了一种可复用的参考架构，并辅以开源实现便于社区验证与扩展。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly operate as execution systems that invoke tools, modify local state, use persistent memory, and interact with external protocols. These capabilities make agents useful, but they also introduce risks related to over-privileged actions, weak auditability, prompt injection, tool poisoning, and uncontrolled side effects. This paper presents Agentao, a governed local-first runtime for tool-using LLM agents. Agentao separates model-generated action proposals from host-authorized execution through a layered architecture consisting of host-facing surfaces, a host contract, a runtime core, a permission-mediated tool system, and supporting subsystems for memory, replay, plugins, skills, sub-agents, and protocol integration. We describe the motivation, threat model, design goals, governance model, execution pipeline, and structured event interface of the system. Agentao does not provide formal safety guarantees; rather, it demonstrates how permissions, state, protocol boundaries, and execution traces can be made explicit runtime abstractions for building agents that are more governable, inspectable, and suitable for host-controlled local environments. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/jin-bo/agentao.

</details>


### [119] [Attributing Preprocessing Invariance in Spectral Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14227)
*Dongjun Wei,Hongyi Wu,Yinuo Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对光谱基础模型中"预处理不变性"的主流衡量方式提出质疑。指出Raman基础模型在施加任何可学习参数之前就会对输入做归一化；当归一化本身就能把不同方式预处理的光谱映射到同一向量时，所谓的不变性并非来自学习。实验表明，在六个Raman数据集上，冻结模型并不显著优于其自身的归一化（后者无任何可学习参数）。训练确实改善了编码器，但受控实验显示模型只在变换"到达"输入时才学会忽略它。跨五个模态的多个已发布系统中，大多数归一化已能消除该类变换，且有系统将此不变性归功于学习。复现其中两个系统显示无额外收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预处理不变性被视作光谱基础模型的重要目标：冻结模型应能适应实验室不同的预处理流程。传统衡量方式是在一种预处理下训练分类器、在另一种预处理下测试，若精度保持则视为模型学到了不变性。作者认为该解读有缺陷，因为归一化发生在任何可学习参数之前，可能已独自完成映射，使不变性与学习无关。

Method: 以Raman基础模型为案例，分析其归一化步骤：使用每条光谱自身统计量的归一化，恰好当一条光谱是另一条的正倍数加常数时，会把不同预处理的谱图映射到同一向量，而多种标准预处理操作恰属此类。作者将编码器与其归一化本身（无参数基线）对照衡量，在六个Raman数据集上比较；并通过受控实验考察模型是否仅在变换到达编码器时才学会忽略它；还设计数值测试以判定给定归一化能消除哪些变换；最后在五个模态的已发布系统中复现比较。

Result: 六个Raman评价集上，模型并未可测量地优于其归一化本身；模型在原始谱上确有改进，但归一化单独也能实现同样的改进。训练确实比随机初始化提升了编码器，但受控实验表明只有变换直接作用于编码器输入时它才学会忽视该变换。数值测试可明确判定归一化能消除哪些变换。跨五个模态的多数已发布系统中，归一化已能消除该形式变换，却仍被若干系统宣称是学习到的不变性；复现其中两个系统未见额外收益。

Conclusion: 光谱基础模型的预处理不变性在很大程度上可能源自预置的输入归一化，而非可学习编码器。评测不变性时应以归一化本身作为无参数基线对照，否则会高估学习贡献；即便训练带来编码器改进，也不能把归一化天然完成的不变性归因于学习。

Abstract: Preprocessing invariance is an appealing goal for spectral foundation models: a frozen model should remain useful when laboratories preprocess spectra differently. It is usually measured by training a classifier under one preprocessing pipeline and testing it under another, with preserved accuracy read as evidence of learning. We revisit that reading, using a Raman foundation model as a case study. Such models normalize their inputs before any learned parameter is applied. If that normalization maps two differently preprocessed spectra to the same vector, the encoder receives identical inputs, so the invariance cannot be attributed to learning. For a normalization that uses each spectrum's own statistics, this happens exactly when one spectrum is a positive multiple of the other plus a constant. Several standard preprocessing operations take that form. The encoder should therefore be measured against the normalization alone, which has no learned parameters. On six Raman evaluation datasets, the model does not measurably outperform its own normalization. It improves on raw spectra, but so does the normalization alone. Training does improve the encoder over random initialization, and a controlled experiment shows that it learns to ignore a transformation only when that transformation reaches it. A numerical test settles which transformations a given normalization removes. Across released systems in five modalities, most normalizations already remove transformations of that form, and several of those systems claim that invariance as learned. Replicating the comparison on two of them shows no gain either.

</details>


### [120] [AI Evaluation Should Work With Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13577)
*Jan Kulveit,Gavin Leech,Tomáš Gavenčiak,Raymond Douglas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，指出当前以"超人自主表现"为核心的 AI 评估范式，隐含着取代人类的目标，正把 AI 发展引向错误方向。作者主张应转向评估人机协作团队（human–AI teams）的整体表现，认为这种协作导向的转变将促使 AI 系统真正成为人类能力的补充，从而带来比现有范式更优的社会结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者认为主流 AI 评估范式过度聚焦于"超人式的自主表现"，其隐含目标是用机器取代人类，这导致开发方向与社会福祉相悖。为了纠正这一偏差，有必要重新定义评估的核心对象与价值导向。

Method: 采用立场论证（position paper）的方式，通过批判现有评估范式的导向性缺陷，提出"以人机团队绩效为评估对象"的替代框架，并从社会结果的角度论证其合理性与优越性。

Result: 论文并未给出实证实验结果，而是提出一种理念层面的转向主张：将 AI 评估从单一智能体的自主能力评测，转向人机协作团队的整体表现评估，以培育真正互补于人类能力的 AI 系统。

Conclusion: AI 评估范式的协作化转变（从替代人类转向增强人类）将引导 AI 系统成为人类能力的真正补充，进而带来远比现行范式更好的社会结果。

Abstract: This position paper argues that the dominant paradigm of AI evaluation (which focuses on superhuman autonomous performance and so implicitly targets the goal of replacing humans) is guiding AI development in the wrong direction. Instead, the AI community should pivot to evaluating the performance of human--AI teams. We argue that this collaborative shift will foster AI systems that act as true complements to human capabilities and therefore lead to far better societal outcomes than will the current process.

</details>


### [121] [Stable Miscalibration in Large Language Models: A Practical View of High-Confidence Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13591)
*Akira Okutomi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出"稳定误校准"假说：大型语言模型中的高置信错误可能并非源于脆弱的内部推理，而是在小扰动下局部稳定。作者结合输出层面的审计分数与内部隐藏状态敏感性探针，发现自批判提示能够跨层降低隐藏状态敏感性，支持提示诱导的局部稳定化；但审计定义的高置信错误与高置信正确回答在局部敏感性上并无显著差异，说明部分高置信错误可能是稳定且误校准的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的高置信错误通常被视为内部推理脆弱的证据。作者质疑这一默认解读，提出另一种可能：这些错误恰是稳定误校准（stable miscalibration）的体现——即一个自信且错误的答案在微小扰动下仍保持局部稳定。区分这两种解释对于理解模型内部机制与校准问题至关重要。

Method: 结合两种诊断工具：(1) 输出层面的标签感知审计分数，在强迫回答基线下按置信度变化与过度自信错误对领域进行排序；(2) 内部敏感性探针，测量隐藏状态在扰动下的移动程度。实验在多领域二元事实审计集上进行，并在三个开放权重模型中检验自批判提示对分层隐藏状态敏感性的影响。

Result: 审计分数能够追踪"弃权感知的自批判"降低决策损失的领域，但直接带有标签的基线对相同增益的排序更强。在内部层面，自批判提示在三个开放权重模型中一致地降低了各层的隐藏状态敏感性。然而，审计定义的高置信错误并不比高置信正确回答更局部敏感。

Conclusion: 结果支持自批判提示引发的是"提示诱导的局部稳定化"，而非单纯的输出层面弃权模式；但这并不意味着校准。部分高置信错误可能既是稳定的又是误校准的，而非简单地反映脆弱的内部推理。这提醒我们，置信度稳定性与正确性校准是需要分别考察的两个维度。

Abstract: High-confidence errors in large language models are often treated as evidence of fragile internal inference. We study a different possibility: stable miscalibration, where a confident wrong answer remains locally stable under small perturbations. We combine two diagnostics: a label-aware output-level audit score that ranks domains by confidence variation and overconfident mistakes under a forced-answer baseline, and an internal sensitivity probe that measures hidden-state movement. On a multi-domain binary factual audit set, this audit score tracks where abstention-aware self-critique reduces decision loss, although direct labeled baselines rank the same gain more strongly. Internally, self-critical prompting consistently reduces hidden-state sensitivity across layers in three open-weight models. This supports prompt-induced local stabilization rather than a purely output-level abstention pattern, but it does not imply calibration: audit-defined overconfident errors are not clearly more locally sensitive than confidently correct answers, so some high-confidence errors may be stable and miscalibrated rather than simply fragile.

</details>


### [122] [Measuring Cross-Task Behavioral Consistency in Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13598)
*Amritesh Banerjee,Pranil Raichura*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出行为一致性度量（BCM），通过训练模型从智能体执行轨迹的行为特征预测任务成功率，并计算逐轨迹特征归因向量之间的平均成对相似度，从而量化智能体在任务间行为的一致性。基于约9000条轨迹、六个语言模型智能体的软件工程任务实验，发现跨任务与任务内一致性是两个可分离的维度，且一致性无法由成功率简化，可作为补充结果指标的过程级可靠性信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估几乎仅依赖成功率等结果指标，只能刻画"能否成功"，却无法反映行为的一致性。作者认为行为一致性是独立、可度量的属性，且以往工作仅测量同一任务的重复性，无法观察到跨任务与任务内一致性之间的分离现象，因此需要新的度量来弥补这一空白。

Method: BCM的具体流程为：1）以智能体的执行轨迹行为特征为输入，训练一个模型预测任务成功与否；2）从该模型为每条轨迹导出特征归因向量；3）在智能体系统内部计算这些向量两两之间的平均相似度作为一致性分数。实验在约9000条轨迹上对六个语言模型智能体在软件工程任务中展开。

Result: 核心发现包括：跨任务一致性与任务内一致性是相互独立的轴，可以出现分歧——某些系统在单任务重复尝试上表现局部可复现，却缺乏全局稳定的跨任务策略；一致性无法归约为成功率，成功率相近的系统在一致性上可能差异显著；并且在控制任务难度不变的任务内对照下，前沿模型与开源模型之间的一致性差距依然存在。

Conclusion: 作者将BCM定位为一种补充结果指标的过程级可靠性信号，并明确阐释了其适用条件。该工作论证了行为一致性作为独立评估维度的重要性，提示仅关注成功率的评估体系会遗漏智能体的过程稳定性信息。

Abstract: Agent evaluation relies almost entirely on outcome metrics such as success rate, which capture whether an agent succeeds but not how consistently it behaves. We argue that behavioral consistency across tasks is a distinct and measurable property, and we introduce the Behavioral Consistency Metric (BCM) to quantify it. BCM trains a model to predict task success from behavioral features of agent execution traces, derives a per-trajectory feature-attribution vector, and measures the mean pairwise similarity of these vectors within an agent system. Across roughly 9,000 trajectories from six language model agents on software engineering tasks, our central finding is that cross-task and within-task consistency are distinct axes that can diverge: some systems are locally reproducible, behaving similarly on repeated attempts at one task, yet globally fragmented, with no stable strategy across different tasks, while others are consistent at both scales. Prior work measures only same-task reproducibility and so cannot observe this separation. We further find that consistency is not reducible to success rate, since systems with comparable success can differ sharply in consistency, and that the frontier-versus-open-source consistency gap persists under a within-task control that holds task difficulty constant. We position BCM as a process-level reliability signal that complements outcome metrics, and we are explicit about the conditions under which it is meaningful.

</details>


### [123] [Cross-Disciplinary Taxonomy and Modeling of Misunderstanding Generation, Amplification, and Detection, from Pragmatics to AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13604)
*Babak Abbaschian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对 AI 中介通信中误解现象的检测问题，提出一个分层过程模型：分歧产生后可能被放大，随后或被检测修复、或持续不被察觉。作者整合九个互不引用的研究领域，识别出十一种精确的失败模式，并依据其在沟通过程中的位置归纳出八个分析层；其中八个机制主要产生分歧、两个放大既有分歧、一个决定分歧是否被检测与修复。作者将八层模型形式化，把信息与传播理论从信号传输扩展到意义重建，并提供逐源证据矩阵、编码手册和九个对话案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着通信从实时的面对面互动转向 AI 中介渠道，交流者失去了传统修复机制所依赖的资源，而新的检测手段尚未跟上，误解的检测成为亟待解决的问题。

Method: 综合九个通常互不引用的研究领域的文献，识别出十一种失败模式，并依据其作用位置推导出八个分析层；对八层模型进行形式化建模，将信息与传播理论从信号传输扩展到意义重建；同时提供逐源证据矩阵（保证可审计性）、编码手册及九个经分析对话案例。

Result: 得到十一种失败模式与八个分析层：其中八个机制主要产生分歧，两个主要放大已有分歧，一个决定分歧是否被检测并修复。每类机制作用于传播过程中的特定位置而非任意节点，并配有可审计的证据矩阵和案例支撑。

Conclusion: 尚无先前的误解分类能够同时将机制定位于沟通过程的具体节点，并按功能对机制进行类型划分；本文的分层模型填补了该空白。

Abstract: Detection of misunderstanding is an urgent problem to solve because communication has moved away from real-time, in-person interaction and is increasingly handled by AI-mediated channels. This shift cuts communicators off from the resources repair depends on faster than new means of detection are being built. In this paper we analyse misunderstanding as a layered process in which a divergence is generated, may then be amplified, and is either detected and repaired or left to persist unnoticed. Consolidating accounts from nine fields of research that do not ordinarily cite one another, we identify eleven exact failure modes and show that each operates at a specific point in a communicative process rather than anywhere within it. Those points give eight analytical layers, derived from the literature rather than adopted from an existing model. Eight of the mechanisms primarily generate a divergence, two primarily amplify one already present, and one governs whether a divergence is detected and repaired. We model the eight layers formally, extending information and communication theory from the transmission of signals to the reconstruction of meaning, and we supply a source-by-source evidence matrix that makes every rating auditable, a coding manual, and nine analysed dialogue cases. No prior classification of misunderstanding both locates mechanisms at points in the process and types them by function.

</details>


### [124] [Active Perception for Embodied Disambiguation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13605)
*Yiwei Liu,Luwei Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于具身目标消歧的主动感知框架：以主动观察作为信息获取的核心手段，由视觉-语言模型根据累积的视觉证据与交互信息，决策是继续观察、请求澄清还是完成目标选择；真实机器人实验表明该框架能在统一流程中同时进行物理信息获取与用户意图澄清。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身环境中目标歧义不仅源于用户意图不明确，还可能源于当前观测缺少任务相关的物理证据（如遮挡、视角受限、文字不可读、目标未被观察到）。现有交互式消歧方法主要依赖向用户提问来获取额外信息，无法处理这些需要机器人主动改变观测视角的情形。

Method: 提出主动感知框架：以主动观察作为信息获取的骨干，利用视觉-语言模型综合累积的视觉证据和交互信息，在"继续观察/请求澄清/完成目标选择"三种动作间做出决策。主动观察既能直接恢复缺失的判别性证据，也能揭示物体名称、标签和语义属性，从而在必要时提升用户澄清的效果。

Result: 真实机器人实验验证了该框架的有效性，表明它能将物理信息获取与用户意图澄清统一到同一个具身消歧流程中。

Conclusion: 主动观察与用户澄清可以在统一的具身消歧框架内互补结合：物理证据的获取能减少不必要的提问，而保留的澄清环节可处理仍存在的意图歧义，从而提升目标选择的可靠性。

Abstract: Natural language provides robots with a flexible task interface, but target ambiguity in embodied environments arises not only from user intent; it can also result from missing taskrelevant physical evidence in the current observation. Existing interactive disambiguation methods primarily obtain additional information by asking the user, whereas occlusion, restricted viewpoints, unreadable text, and unobserved targets require the robot to actively change its observation. We propose an active-perception framework for embodied target disambiguation that uses active observation as the backbone for information acquisition and uses a vision-language model to decide, on the basis of accumulated visual evidence and interaction information, whether to continue observing, request clarification, or complete target selection. Active observation can both directly recover missing discriminative evidence and reveal object names, labels, and semantic attributes, thereby improving user clarification when it remains necessary. Real-robot experiments show that the framework combines physical information acquisition and userintent clarification within a unified embodied disambiguation process.

</details>


### [125] [MobileMem: Learning from a Year of Mobile Experiences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13606)
*Xinle Deng,Yida Xue,Xiangyuan Ru,Haoming Xu,Shuofei Qiao,Mengru Wang,Yijun Chen,Buqiang Xu,Chen Jiang,Yuchen Eleanor Jiang,Lizhong Wang,Jianfeng Wang,Li Zeng,Haofen Wang,Guilin Qi,Huajun Chen,Ningyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MobileMem 是一个面向设备端（on-device）长期记忆研究的基准测试与框架，基于一年时间跨度的移动端体验数据，通过知识驱动的合成流水线构建连贯且时间一致的长时程轨迹，并支持文本与多模态设置，涵盖多跳推理、时间推理、知识更新与隐式偏好推断，使智能体能够记忆过去、理解当下并适应未来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新一代 AI 智能体正从回答孤立问题的系统演变为能够理解、记忆并不断从用户经历中学习的持久化个人助手。这类助手需要长期记忆来积累和利用用户特定经验，但现有基准无法胜任真实的移动端场景——这些场景中的经验具有异构、多模态、持续演化且高度个性化的特点。

Method: MobileMem 提出一种基于知识驱动的合成流水线，从用户与应用会话（user-app sessions）中构建连贯且时间一致的长时程轨迹（long-horizon trajectories）。该基准提供互补的文本与多模态设置，覆盖多跳推理、时间推理、知识更新和隐式偏好推断等任务，强调对经验（experiences）而非孤立事实（isolated facts）进行建模。

Result: MobileMem 提供了年度规模（year-scale）的移动体验数据集合与相应的评估设置，使智能体能够在记忆过去、理解现在、适应未来三个维度上得到系统评测，将长期记忆从单纯的信息检索推进到经验智能（experiential intelligence）层面。

Conclusion: 通过建模经验而非孤立事实，MobileMem 将记忆研究从信息检索推向经验智能，为设备端个人助手在真实移动环境中的长期记忆能力提供了新的基准与研究方向。

Abstract: The next generation of AI agents is increasingly moving beyond systems that answer isolated questions toward persistent personal assistants that can understand, remember, and continuously learn from users' experiences. Such assistants require long-term memory to accumulate and leverage user-specific experiences over time, yet existing benchmarks remain inadequate for realistic mobile settings, where experiences are heterogeneous, multimodal, evolving, and deeply personal. We introduce MobileMem, a benchmark and framework for studying on-device long-term memory, grounded in a year-scale collection of mobile experiences. MobileMem employs a knowledge-grounded synthesis pipeline to construct coherent and temporally consistent long-horizon trajectories from user-app sessions. It provides complementary text and multimodal settings covering multi-hop and temporal reasoning, knowledge updating, and implicit preference inference. Specifically, MobileMem enables agents to remember the past, understand the present, and adapt to the future. By modeling experiences rather than isolated facts, MobileMem moves memory beyond information retrieval toward experiential intelligence for continuous personal learning.

</details>


### [126] [No Universal Signal Predicts Sample-Level LLM Regression under Version Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13607)
*Jia Sheng,Yiwei Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究如何在前沿大语言模型（LLM）更新后，利用推理时可获得的信号预测"样本级回归"（即旧模型回答正确而新模型回答错误的情况）。通过统一的新增价值检验框架，对比单模型信号（置信度、logit 边际、注意力熵）与跨版本信号（输出 KL 散度、似然漂移、token 级 KL、表征漂移），在六个基准、三个任务族、六组模型更新对上发现：信号有效性高度依赖任务与模型更新，且部分跨版本信号在置信度失效时仍有效，可用于将高风险样本回退到旧模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿 LLM 频繁更新并通常在整体上优于旧版，但整体提升无法反映样本级表现：更新可能导致某些样本从正确变为错误（回归）。预测这类回归对可靠部署至关重要，但现有研究缺乏对各类可用信号的系统比较，也没有检验它们相对置信度基线的增量价值。

Method: 提出统一的"附加价值检验"（added-value test），用于隔离每个信号相对置信度基线的增量收益；系统比较单模型信号（置信度、logit 边际、注意力熵）与跨版本信号（输出 KL 散度、似然漂移、token 级 KL、表征漂移）；实验覆盖六个基准、三个任务族（多项选择问答 MCQ、数学推理、代码生成）和六组模型更新对，并设计一个无标签条件下选择高回归风险样本回退旧模型的 proof-of-concept 选择性回退方案。

Result: （1）信号有效性依赖任务：置信度在 MCQ 和较简单数学上最强，而似然/KL 信号在较难数学与代码生成上带来最多增益；（2）没有任何信号在所有模型更新上都普遍最优；（3）某些跨版本信号在置信度失效时仍保持信息量，且无需标签即可获得，支持将高风险样本路由回旧模型的选择性回退。

Conclusion: 研究表明回归预测信号的选择应基于任务级模式，实践者可根据具体更新场景挑选值得信赖的信号；跨版本信号可作为置信度失败时的可靠补充，支持模型更新后的安全部署与选择性回退策略。

Abstract: Frontier LLMs are updated frequently and typically outperform their predecessors in aggregate. But aggregate gains say little about individual samples: an update can still cause sample-level regression, where a response correct under the old model becomes incorrect under the new one. This paper studies how to predict such regressions from signals available at inference time. We compare single-model signals (confidence, logit margin, attention entropy) against cross-version signals (output KL divergence, likelihood drift, token-level KL, representation drift) under a unified added-value test that isolates each signal's gain over a confidence baseline. Across six benchmarks in three task families (multiple-choice question answering, or MCQ; math reasoning; code generation) and six model update pairs, we find that (1) signal effectiveness is task-dependent: confidence is strongest on MCQ and simpler math, while likelihood/KL signals give the most frequent gains on harder math and code; (2) no signal is universally best across model updates either; and (3) some cross-version signals stay informative even when confidence fails, including without labels, which supports a proof-of-concept selective fallback that routes high-risk samples back to the old model. Practitioners can use these task-level patterns to choose which regression signal to trust for a given update. Code is available at https://github.com/jiashengsally/llm-regression-signals.

</details>


### [127] [Evaluating Agentic Learning Harness Capabilities Without Labels via the Scaling Hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13608)
*Aryan Luthra,Kshitij Jain,Siddharth Arya,Bobby Filar,Anna Bertiger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需标注基准即可端到端评估智能体式持续性学习增强系统（Continual Learning Harnesses）的框架：以更强的教师模型提供稀疏修正信号，通过学生模型随时间向教师收敛的程度来量化增强系统的提升，并在网络安全任务中验证该教师相对提升可作为真实提升的有效代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络安全运营场景中，传统以标注基准评估增强系统的方式并不可行：基准标签稀缺、过时且不具代表性，实践者无法判断某个增强系统是否有效或两者孰优；传统LLM-as-a-judge因自身能力不高于被评对象而无法提供有效信号；基于知识蒸馏的方法在标签稀疏、零散且有偏时也不可靠。

Method: 以缩放假设（scaling hypothesis）为理论基础，构建无标注基准的端到端评估框架：更强的教师模型向配备持续性学习增强系统的较小学生模型提供稀疏采样的修正，通过学生随时间向教师收敛的程度对增强系统打分。在多种安全任务、模型家族与增强系统设计下，检验教师相对提升与面向保留金标准提升之间的相关性。&ltbr&gt

Result: 在安全任务、模型家族与增强系统设计等维度上，相对于教师的提升与相对于保留金标准的提升显著相关，验证了教师相对提升（teacher-relative lift）在无标注情形下作为真实增强提升代理的有效性；同时证实能力相近模型之间的LLM-as-a-judge无法产生可用信号。

Conclusion: 教师相对提升可替代标注基准用于评估学习增强系统；当人类以相同的稀疏、高精度修正方式提供反馈时，教师规模的模型可通过同一增强系统获得改进，说明该框架既可评估又可迁移到训练教师模型自身。

Abstract: Agentic "Continual Learning Harnesses", systems that pair an LLM with retrieval or memory to improve from feedback without retraining, have shown growing value in cybersecurity. But their value is conventionally measured by gains against labeled benchmarks, an approach that often fails in operational security settings. Benchmark labels are scarce, stale, and unrepresentative, so a practitioner often cannot tell whether a given harness helps at all or which of two is better for their task. Traditional LLM-as-a-judge offers little signal because it is no stronger than the agent it evaluates, and distillation is unreliable on scarce, sporadic, and biased labels.
  We propose a framework for evaluating learning harnesses end-to-end without a labeled benchmark, grounded in the scaling hypothesis. A stronger teacher model provides sparsely sampled corrections to a smaller student with a continual learning harness. We score a harness by how much its student converges toward the teacher over time. Across security tasks, model families, and harness designs, we show that improvement relative to the teacher correlates with improvement relative to a held-out gold standard, validating teacher-relative lift as a proxy for true harness uplift when labels are absent. We further show that LLM-as-a-judge between similarly powered models yields no usable signal. These results suggest that a teacher-sized model can be improved through the same harness when humans provide the same kind of sparse, high-precision corrections.

</details>


### [128] [SemPlan: Benchmarking Structured Semantic Planning for LLM-Based Queries over Enterprise Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13612)
*Bruno Santos Teixeira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SemPlan Benchmark 是一个确定性的合成双语基准（英语与巴西葡萄牙语，共1,800个案例，其中1,200个构成冻结的科学评测子集），用于评估面向企业数据的自然语言接口架构。在同一模型配置下比较了四种架构：直接SQL生成（A1）、有界工具智能体（A2）、结构化语义请求+确定性规划执行（A3）、以及带澄清/有状态语义规划的变体（A4）。4,800条主记录上答案正确率均较低（A1:22.25%、A2:22.58%、A3:25.67%、A4:24.25%），A3正确率最高且显著优于其余三者；A1保留最高的策略合规率和最低的不安全/无效率；A4拥有最低平均API成本与最低错误拒绝率；150例稳定性子集上的可重复性为92.00%~98.67%。结论支持"权衡"而非"单一最优排名"的解释：额外的结构约束改变了失败模式与效率，但并未单调提升正确率，也未解决歧义与多轮状态一致性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向企业数据的自然语言接口需要把模糊、未充分指定的请求转化为受治理、可执行的系统行为，同时控制无效查询、策略违规、成本与非确定性。研究者希望用一个可控、确定性的基准来系统评估这一架构设计空间，而非依赖非确定性的大模型端到端评测。

Method: 构建SemPlan Benchmark：合成、确定性、双语的1,800个案例（英/葡），其中1,200个为冻结的科学评测子集；在同一模型配置下对比四种架构——A1（直接SQL生成）、A2（有界工具智能体）、A3（结构化语义请求生成+确定性规划与执行）、A4（澄清式/有状态语义规划变体）；并预先设定配对正确性分析、策略正确率、不安全/无效率、API成本、错误拒绝率以及150例稳定性子集上的重复性度量。

Result: 4,800条主记录中，答案正确率绝对水平较低：A1为22.25%、A2为22.58%、A3为25.67%、A4为24.25%。A3取得最高正确率，并在预先指定的配对正确性分析中显著超过A1、A2、A4；A1的策略正确率最高且不安全/无效率最低；A4的平均API成本与错误拒绝率最低；150例稳定性子集上答案正确的可重复性介于92.00%到98.67%之间。

Conclusion: 结果支持"权衡取舍"而非"通用排名"的解释：四种架构各有权衡，不存在单一最优方案。增加结构化约束会改变失败模式与效率特征，但并不能单调地提升正确率，也无法从根本上解决请求歧义和多轮对话中的状态一致性问题。
